Parameter inequalities.

The inequalities used repeatedly in the iteration are

\displaystyle\mathrm{D}_{R}

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{D}_{u},

\displaystyle e_{R}

\displaystyle\leq e_{u},

\displaystyle\Xi e_{u}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq\tau^{-1}\leq\mu\leq\epsilon_{t}^{-1}\leq\accentset{\ast}{\tau}^{-1},

\displaystyle\Xi

\displaystyle\leq\widehat{\Xi}\leq\lambda,

\displaystyle 1

\displaystyle\leq(D_{u}/D_{R})\leq\widehat{C}(D_{u}/D_{R})(N\Xi)^{4\eta}N^{6/L}\leq N.

A.4
Estimates cheat sheet

This table is meant as a reading guide for the estimates used in the proof. We put
{\underline{L}}=L-3
,
\widehat{N}=N^{1/L}
,
\widehat{\Xi}=\widehat{N}\Xi
, and
\lambda\in 2\pi\mathbb{Z},\lambda\sim N\Xi
. Unless a row says otherwise, the implicit constants may depend on
L
,
\eta
, the derivative order, and the fixed cutoffs, but not on
N
,
\Xi
,
D_{u}
, or
D_{R}
. The entries are alphabetized by the left column heading in the alphabetical order for the symbol; for example,
\mathcal{B}
appears before
\gamma
. (Only a few exceptions to this alphabetical ordering occur, for example in “Difference of phase gradients”, for pedagogical reasons.)

Quantity

Estimate and location

Bilinear anti-divergence
{\cal B}_{a}^{ij}

In the proof of Proposition
3.5
for
\bar{r}_{J}
, the high high, high low, and low high pieces of
{\cal B}_{a}^{ij}[z_{J}^{a},\theta_{\epsilon}]
are bounded at size
\widehat{N}^{(|\vec{a}|-{\underline{L}})_{+}}\Xi^{|\vec{a}|}\tau^{-1}S_{r}(1+\mathpzc{h})
. The same proof gives the high frequency version with the additional factor
2^{-\alpha q}\widehat{\Xi}^{\alpha}
after applying
P_{q}
. See the paragraph “The
{\cal B}
term.”

Lower order wave terms
\delta\theta_{I},\delta u_{I}

For
0\leq r\leq 1
, the microlocal remainders satisfy
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}\tilde{D}_{t}^{r}\delta\theta_{I}\|_{C^{0}}+\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}\tilde{D}_{t}^{r}\delta u_{I}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim\lambda^{1/2}N^{-1}\widehat{N}^{(|\vec{a}|+2-{\underline{L}})_{+}}\Xi^{|\vec{a}|}\tau^{-r}D_{R,n}^{1/2}
. For two material derivatives, replace
\tau^{-r}D_{R,n}^{1/2}
by
\epsilon_{t}^{-1}(\Xi e_{u}^{1/2})D_{R,n}^{1/2}
. See (
206
) and (
207
).

Phase exponential
e^{i\lambda\xi_{I}}

The final weighted norm satisfies
\accentset{\ast}{H}^{*}[e^{i\lambda\xi_{I}}]\lesssim 1
. This estimate is used in the nonstationary phase parametrix. See (
248
).

Time envelopes
e_{n}

The envelope satisfies
\|d^{r}e_{n}^{1/2}/dt^{r}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim\tau^{-r}D_{R,n}^{1/2}
. The Newton stress scale is
D_{R,n}=(N^{-\eta}\Xi^{-\eta})^{n}D_{R,0}
. See (
33
) and (
30
).

Amplitudes
\gamma_{I}

For
0\leq r\leq 1
,
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}\tilde{D}_{t}^{r}\gamma_{I}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim\widehat{N}^{(|\vec{a}|+1-{\underline{L}})_{+}}\Xi^{|\vec{a}|}\tau^{-r}D_{R,n}^{1/2}
. Also
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}\tilde{D}_{t}^{2}\gamma_{I}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim\widehat{N}^{(|\vec{a}|+2-{\underline{L}})_{+}}\Xi^{|\vec{a}|}\epsilon_{t}^{-1}(\Xi e_{u}^{1/2})D_{R,n}^{1/2}
. See Proposition
4.5
, especially (
199
).

Final weighted norms
H^{*}

The norms associated to
\partial_{t}
,
\overline{D}_{t}
,
\tilde{D}_{t}
, and
\accentset{\ast}{D}_{t}
are comparable:
\accentset{\ast}{H}^{*}[F]\lesssim H^{*}[F]\lesssim\bar{H}^{*}[F]\lesssim\tilde{H}^{*}[F]\lesssim\accentset{\ast}{H}^{*}[F]
. They also satisfy
\mathring{H}^{*}[FG]\lesssim\mathring{H}^{*}[F]\mathring{H}^{*}[G]
. See Proposition
5.1
and (
215
).

Mixed derivatives
H_{\zeta,M}^{(R)}

If
\zeta\geq\Xi e_{u}^{1/2}
, then
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}\overline{D}_{t}\nabla_{\vec{b}}F\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim\widehat{N}^{(|\vec{a}|+|\vec{b}|+1-L)_{+}}\Xi^{|\vec{a}|+|\vec{b}|}\zeta H_{\zeta,M}^{(1)}[F]
. See Lemma
3.1
.

Weighted norms
\mathring{H}_{\zeta}

For
\mathring{D}_{t}\in\{\partial_{t},\overline{D}_{t},\tilde{D}_{t}\}
, the weighted norms are comparable, and the product and chain rules hold:
\mathring{H}_{\zeta}[FG]\lesssim\mathring{H}_{\zeta}[F]\mathring{H}_{\zeta}[G]
and
\mathring{H}_{\zeta}[G(F)]\lesssim(1+\mathring{H}_{\zeta}[F])^{L^{\prime}}
. See Lemma
4.1
and Proposition
4.1
.

Parameter hierarchy

We use
\mu=N^{1/2}\Xi e_{R}^{1/2}
,
\tau=b(\log\widehat{\Xi})^{-1}(\Xi e_{u}^{1/2})^{-1}
,
\widetilde{e}_{u}^{1/2}=(\log\widehat{\Xi})e_{u}^{1/2}
with
\Xi\widetilde{e}_{u}^{1/2}=b\tau^{-1}
,
\epsilon_{t}=(\Xi e_{u}^{1/2})^{-1}(D_{u}/D_{R})^{-1/2}N^{-1/2}
, and
\accentset{\ast}{\tau}^{-1}=(N\Xi)^{3/2}D_{R}^{1/2}
. The useful chain is
\Xi e_{u}^{1/2}\leq\tau^{-1}\leq\mu\leq\epsilon_{t}^{-1}\leq\accentset{\ast}{\tau}^{-1}
. See (
14
)–(
17
), Proposition
3.7
, and the parameter list in the glossary.

Frequency growth parameter
N

The Main Lemma assumes
N\geq\widehat{C}N^{6/L}N^{4\eta}\Xi^{4\eta}(D_{u}/D_{R})
. This lower bound is used when absorbing the Newton gluing error and later when comparing the final time scale. See (
7
).

Difference of phase gradients
\nabla\check{\xi}-\nabla\xi

The Newton and convex integration phase gradients satisfy
\|\nabla\check{\xi}(t)-\nabla\xi_{I}(t)\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim N^{-1/2}(D_{u}/D_{R})^{-1/2}
on the support of the wave. See (
198
).

Nonstationary phase
Q^{j\ell}

If
G=e^{i\lambda\xi_{I}}g
has mean zero, then there is a traceless symmetric
Q^{j\ell}
with
\nabla_{j}\nabla_{\ell}Q^{j\ell}=G
and
\tilde{H}^{*}[Q]\leq C_{D}((N\Xi)^{-2}+N^{-D/2})\tilde{H}^{\prime\prime}_{2D+L}[g]
. See Proposition “Nonstationary phase” and (
240
).

Zero order multipliers and commutators

A degree zero multiplier
{\cal Q}
obeys
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}P_{q}{\cal Q}F\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim 2^{-\alpha q}\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}F\|_{\dot{C}^{\alpha}}
and
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}{\cal Q}F\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim(\log\widehat{\Xi})\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}F\|_{C^{0}}+\widehat{\Xi}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}F\|_{\dot{C}^{\alpha}}
. The commutator with
\overline{D}_{t}
is estimated in Lemma
3.6
. See Proposition
3.6
and Lemma
3.6
.

Coarse stress
R_{\epsilon}

The spatially mollified stress satisfies
\|R-R_{\epsilon}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim N^{-1}D_{R}
,
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}R_{\epsilon}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim_{\vec{a}}\widehat{N}^{(|\vec{a}|-L)_{+}}\Xi^{|\vec{a}|}D_{R}
, and
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}\overline{D}_{t}R_{\epsilon}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim_{\vec{a}}(\Xi e_{u}^{1/2})\widehat{N}^{(|\vec{a}|+1-L)_{+}}\Xi^{|\vec{a}|}D_{R}
. See the end of the subsection “Regularizing the scalar field and error tensor.”

Gluing stress
R_{(n+1)}

The Newton gluing stress satisfies
\|R_{(n+1)}\|_{C^{0}}\leq D_{R,n+1}
,
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}R_{(n+1)}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim_{\vec{a}}\widehat{N}^{(|\vec{a}|-L)_{+}}\Xi^{|\vec{a}|}D_{R,n+1}
, and
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}\overline{D}_{t}R_{(n+1)}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim_{\vec{a}}\widehat{N}^{(|\vec{a}|+1-L)_{+}}\Xi^{|\vec{a}|}(\Xi e_{u}^{1/2})D_{R,n+1}
. See Proposition
3.1
and (
75
)–(
77
).

High frequency error
R_{H}

After nonstationary phase,
\tilde{H}^{*}[R_{H}]\lesssim(\lambda^{-2}+N^{-D/2})(\lambda\Xi D_{R})
. With
D
chosen depending on
\eta
, this gives
\tilde{H}^{*}[R_{H}]\lesssim D_{R}/N
. See Section
8.1
.

Newton quadratic and mollification errors
R_{Q,n},R_{M,n}

The Newton errors satisfy
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}R_{M,n}\|_{C^{0}}+\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}R_{Q,n}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim(N\Xi)^{|\vec{a}|}N^{-1}D_{R}
and the corresponding pure time derivative bounds cost
\accentset{\ast}{\tau}^{-1}
. See Proposition
3.7
.

Stress error
R_{S}

The flow and stress error obeys
\accentset{\ast}{H}^{*}[R_{S}]\lesssim(D_{u}/D_{R})^{-1/2}N^{-1/2}D_{R}
. The microlocal remainder
\delta B_{I}
contributes
N^{-1}D_{R}
. See Section
7
.

Transport error
R_{T}

The transport error satisfies
\tilde{H}^{*}[R_{T}]\lesssim(N\Xi)^{-2}\mu\lambda^{1/2}D_{R}^{1/2}\sim N^{-1}D_{R}
. See Section
8.1
.

Mollified stress
\tilde{R}_{n}

Flow mollification gives
\|R_{(n)}-\tilde{R}_{n}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim N^{-1/2}(D_{u}/D_{R})^{-1/2}D_{R,n}
,
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}\tilde{R}_{n}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim_{\vec{a}}\widehat{N}^{(|\vec{a}|-{\underline{L}})_{+}}\Xi^{|\vec{a}|}D_{R,n}
,
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}\tilde{D}_{t}\tilde{R}_{n}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim_{\vec{a}}(\Xi e_{u}^{1/2})\widehat{N}^{(|\vec{a}|+1-{\underline{L}})_{+}}\Xi^{|\vec{a}|}D_{R,n}
, and the second material derivative costs
\epsilon_{t}^{-1}(\Xi e_{u}^{1/2})\widehat{N}^{(|\vec{a}|+1-{\underline{L}})_{+}}\Xi^{|\vec{a}|}D_{R,n}
. See Proposition
4.3
and (
187
).

Error list for
\accentset{\ast}{R}

After adding
\Theta
, the new stress is decomposed as
\accentset{\ast}{R}=R_{T}+R_{H}+R_{M}+R_{S}+P_{(\Gamma)}+R_{(\Gamma)}
. The defining equations for
R_{T}
,
R_{H}
,
R_{M}
, and
R_{S}
appear in (
224
). The Summary Section before the Overall gameplan lists where the estimates for these pieces are checked.

Trace free Newton anti-divergence
\rho_{J}

On
\operatorname{supp}\tilde{\chi}_{k}^{\prime}(t)
,
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}\overline{D}_{t}^{r}\rho_{J}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim_{\vec{a}}\widehat{N}^{(|\vec{a}|+r-{\underline{L}})_{+}}\Xi^{|\vec{a}|}\tau^{-r}S_{\rho}
for
0\leq r\leq 1
. See (
74
).

Size table
S_{F}

The assigned sizes are
S_{w}=\Xi^{2}\mu^{-1}D_{R,n}
,
S_{z}=\Xi\mu^{-1}D_{R,n}
,
S_{r}=\mu^{-1}D_{R,n}
,
S_{u}=(\log\widehat{\Xi})\Xi^{2}\mu^{-1}D_{R,n}
, and
S_{\rho}=(\log\widehat{\Xi})\mu^{-1}D_{R,n}
. See (
3.7
).

Microlocal wave
\Theta_{I}

For each wave,
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}\Theta_{I}\|_{C^{0}}+\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}T^{\ell}\Theta_{I}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim_{\vec{a}}\lambda^{|\vec{a}|+1/2}D_{R}^{1/2}
and
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}|\nabla|^{-1/2}\Theta_{I}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim_{\vec{a}}\lambda^{|\vec{a}|}D_{R}^{1/2}
. See (
211
) and (
212
).

Coarse field
\theta_{\epsilon},u_{\epsilon}

For the spatial mollification scale
\epsilon=N^{-1/L}\Xi^{-1}
,
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}\theta_{\epsilon}\|_{C^{0}}+\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}u_{\epsilon}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim_{\vec{a}}\widehat{N}^{(|\vec{a}|-L)_{+}}\Xi^{|\vec{a}|}e_{u}^{1/2}
, and
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}\overline{D}_{t}\theta_{\epsilon}\|_{C^{0}}+\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}\overline{D}_{t}u_{\epsilon}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim_{\vec{a}}\widehat{N}^{(|\vec{a}|+1-L)_{+}}\Xi^{|\vec{a}|+1}e_{u}
. See (
63
) and (
64
).

Difference
\theta-\theta_{\epsilon},u-u_{\epsilon}

For
0\leq|\vec{a}|+r\leq L
and
0\leq r\leq 1
,
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}\partial_{t}^{r}(\theta-\theta_{\epsilon})\|_{C^{0}}+\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}\partial_{t}^{r}(u-u_{\epsilon})\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim(N\Xi)^{|\vec{a}|}(\accentset{\ast}{\Xi}\accentset{\ast}{e}_{u}^{1/2})^{r}N^{-1}e_{u}^{1/2}
. See Lemma
3.7
.

Final fields
\accentset{\ast}{\theta},\accentset{\ast}{u}

The final weighted norm obeys
\accentset{\ast}{H}^{*}[\accentset{\ast}{u}]\lesssim(N\Xi)^{1/2}D_{R}^{1/2}
and
\accentset{\ast}{H}^{*}[\accentset{\ast}{\theta}]\lesssim(N\Xi)^{1/2}D_{R}^{1/2}
. See Proposition
5.2
.

Regularity iteration

The limiting construction gives
\||\nabla|^{-1/2}(\theta-\theta_{0})\|_{C^{0}}\leq C_{\delta}D_{R,0}^{1/2}
and
|\nabla|^{-1/2}\theta\in C^{1/2-2\delta}
. The parameter recursion uses (
278
). See Theorem
4
.

Newton velocity
u_{J},u_{n}

The velocity produced by a Newton correction satisfies
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}u_{n}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim_{\vec{a}}\widehat{N}^{(|\vec{a}|-{\underline{L}})_{+}}\Xi^{|\vec{a}|}S_{u}
. Also
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}\overline{D}_{t}^{r}u_{n}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim_{\vec{a}}\widehat{N}^{(r+|\vec{a}|-{\underline{L}})_{+}}\Xi^{|\vec{a}|}(\Xi e_{u}^{1/2})^{r}e_{u}^{1/2}
for
0\leq r\leq 1
. See (
72
) and (
73
).

Main Lemma correction
W

The correction satisfies
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}|\nabla|^{-1/2}W\|_{C^{0}}\leq\widehat{C}(N\Xi)^{|\vec{a}|}D_{R}^{1/2}
for
|\vec{a}|=0,1
. Its time support obeys
\operatorname{supp}_{t}W\cup\operatorname{supp}_{t}\accentset{\ast}{R}\subseteq N(J_{0})
. See the Main Lemma, (
10
), and (
9
).

Newton fields
w_{J},z_{J},r_{J}

For
F\in{\bf F}_{J}\cup\{w_{J},z_{J},r_{J}\}
,
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}F\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim_{\vec{a}}\widehat{N}^{(|\vec{a}|-{\underline{L}})_{+}}\Xi^{|\vec{a}|}S_{F}
. See Proposition
3.1
and (
70
).

Newton perturbation
w_{n}

The Newton correction satisfies
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}|\nabla|^{-1/2}w_{n}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim\Xi^{|\vec{a}|}D_{R,n}^{1/2}
for
0\leq|\vec{a}|\leq 1
. The rough pure time bounds are
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}\partial_{t}^{r}w_{n}\|_{C^{0}}+\Xi\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}\partial_{t}^{r}z_{n}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim(N\Xi)^{|\vec{a}|}\mu^{r}S_{w}
. See (
71
) and (
147
).

Phase function
\check{\xi}_{J}

The Newton phase gradients satisfy
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}\nabla\check{\xi}_{J}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim_{\vec{a}}\widehat{N}^{(|\vec{a}|+1-L)_{+}}\Xi^{|\vec{a}|}
and
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}\overline{D}_{t}\nabla\check{\xi}_{J}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim_{\vec{a}}\widehat{N}^{(|\vec{a}|+1-L)_{+}}\Xi^{|\vec{a}|+1}e_{u}^{1/2}
. See Proposition
3.2
.

Phase function
\xi_{I}

The convex integration phases satisfy
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}\nabla\xi_{I}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim_{\vec{a}}\widehat{N}^{(|\vec{a}|+1-{\underline{L}})_{+}}\Xi^{|\vec{a}|}
,
\|\nabla_{\vec{a}}\tilde{D}_{t}\nabla\xi_{I}\|_{C^{0}}\lesssim_{\vec{a}}\widehat{N}^{(|\vec{a}|+1-{\underline{L}})_{+}}\Xi^{|\vec{a}|}(\Xi e_{u}^{1/2})
, and a second advective derivative bound with
\widehat{N}^{(|\vec{a}|+2-{\underline{L}})_{+}}(\Xi e_{u}^{1/2})^{2}
. See Proposition
4.4
.
